([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/sutra/commands/commit.md) · ★2)
- **sutra:do**: Do anything across the whole pack. Triggers on "sutra do", "orchestrate", "handle this across the pack", "/sutra:do &lt;anything&gt;". - **sutra:recall**: Reason with prior knowledge across the pack.
